| [ADR-0017](adr/0017-mailbox-and-gate-protocol.md) | Mailbox and gate protocol: an index stack drained whole, no ABA tag, four sequentially consistent operations (amends ADR-0006) |
| [ADR-0018](adr/0018-membrane-integration.md) | Membrane integration: shift leaks with a one-LSB floor, difference coupling, a 2 ms refractory window, apical-gated plateaus, adaptive threshold |
| [ADR-0019](adr/0019-short-term-plasticity.md) | Short-term plasticity: event-driven Tsodyks–Markram on the Q0.8 fields, exponentials by binary exponentiation |
| [ADR-0020](adr/0020-computational-phenomenology-and-synthetic-qualia.md) | Computational phenomenology: the state variables the theories name, as integer rules, and what the document does not claim for them |
| [ADR-0021](adr/0021-native-cognitive-language-and-conceptual-blending.md) | Native cognitive language: nested constructions, conceptual blending, default-mode wandering and dialogue grounding, without a language model |
| [ADR-0022](adr/0022-synapse-fan-out-and-stdp.md) | Synaptic fan-out and STDP: index + 1 chains, synapse tokens, stored releases, spike messages, and the nearest-neighbour pair rule at the presynaptic spike; image format 6 |
| [ADR-0023](adr/0023-executor.md) | The executor: a runtime crate, in-house work-stealing deques, three barrier-separated phases per tick, and the one `unsafe` in the workspace |
| [ADR-0024](adr/0024-cortex-image-and-clock-sweep.md) | The `.cortex` image: section directory, table-free CRC-64/XZ, a read-into-arenas loader and writer, a write-ahead log for the clock sweep, and the Tier-2 delta record; format version 7 |
| [ADR-0025](adr/0025-term-arena-and-unification.md) | A term arena and first-order unification for `cortex-reasoning`: a second record under ADR-0016's test, bindings in a caller's table, a trail undone on failure, bounds that are results |
| [ADR-0026](adr/0026-social-acumen-and-re-representation.md) | Social acumen and re-representation: a sincerity gap and a second-level expectation, tact and indirectness, an anomaly that marks a framework stale and a re-representation, a basis rotation; what is not claimed |
| [ADR-0027](adr/0027-vocal-synthesis-and-computational-humor.md) | Vocal synthesis as motor output and computational humor: a `VocalFrame` with an integer source–filter renderer, the benign-violation appraisal, reward and the playful marker; what is not claimed |
| [ADR-0028](adr/0028-edge-behaviour-audit.md) | Edge behaviour under audit: a gate closed until evaluated (format version 9), a reflex released with its override, every packed or shifted input read within its bound, a loader that refuses what the writer would never produce, quiescence including the injector ring |
| [ADR-0029](adr/0029-structural-enforcement.md) | Structural enforcement of the invariants review held: workspace lints for `unsafe` and floats, a manifest check for dependencies, rustdoc as a gate, the arithmetic lint where it passes, pinned actions, normalised line endings |
| [ADR-0030](adr/0030-verification-governance.md) | Verification governance: a mutation gate on the lines a change touches, a zero-dependency property kit, exhaustive tests where the domain allows, the tests in the engine's profile, a determinism pin checked on two architectures; what is not adopted |
| [ADR-0031](adr/0031-policy-amendment.md) | Self-amendment: a policy amendment on trial in two forks of the image, committed only when the engine behaved exactly as before and cost less; the engine never amends its own code; the amendment register; image format 10 |
| [ADR-0032](adr/0032-three-factor-plasticity.md) | Three-factor plasticity: an eligibility trace per synapse in the block, consolidated into the weight by the modulator at the presynaptic spike; the apical mask in the chain word; the engine's modulator in the executor and the image; format version 11 |
| [ADR-0033](adr/0033-tick-duration-in-the-header.md) | The tick in the image header: its duration, refused when it is not this build's, and the clock at the write, which the loader resumes so that every stamp keeps its meaning |
| [ADR-0034](adr/0034-synaptic-density.md) | Synaptic density: the four-synapse block stands; eight-bit logarithmic weights, eight synapses per block and an in-engine far-memory synapse tier rejected; the density levers in order, each gated by a measurement |
| [ADR-0035](adr/0035-cadence-and-the-population-tally.md) | Multirate stepping as a cadence: a power-of-two period and a phase, a mask on the tick; the population spike tally; the idle-tick benchmark; conservative lookahead left to a measurement |
| [ADR-0036](adr/0036-criticality-control.md) | Criticality control: the branching ratio by lag-one regression over bins of population activity in the homeostasis record, a bounded global synaptic gain as the actuator, stepped once per window and persisted in the image; per-unit scaling Specified; format version 12 |
| [ADR-0037](adr/0037-sleep-regulation.md) | Sleep as a state machine on the window cadence: a two-process sleep pressure against a circadian threshold, three stages under an ultradian budget, a wake as an input; the homeostasis record's sleep fields; format version 13 |
| [ADR-0038](adr/0038-episodic-ledger-and-replay.md) | The episodic ledger and its replay: an append-only second record of `cortex-hippocampus` under ADR-0016's test, tagged as an input, replayed into the network on a ripple cadence in slow-wave sleep and consolidated by the modulator's rule, depotentiated in REM; the canvas hydration Specified; sections 44 and 45 |
| [ADR-0039](adr/0039-hypervector-body.md) | The hypervector body: a second record of `cortex-symbolic` under ADR-0016's test, 160 words of 64 bits, with the vector-symbolic algebra as integer rules over the words (binding, permutation, bundling with a fixed tie-breaker, the Hamming distance, the nearest codebook entry, a decode confidence); no intrinsic, no `unsafe`; the width 10 240 |
| [ADR-0040](adr/0040-categorial-reduction.md) | Categorial reduction on the term arena: a category is a term, the four combinatory rules are unifications, a greedy shift-reduce reducer over caller slices with a log; type raising and a chart Specified; the runtime composes the derivation into a frame, the frame into a hypervector and back |
| [ADR-0041](adr/0041-induction-on-the-term-arena.md) | Induction on the term arena: a definite clause is a term over a reserved functor; the least general generalisation and the inverse-resolution operators as bounded rules over caller slices, a predicate invented from a reserved band, every output resolving back to its inputs; first-fit matching |
| [ADR-0042](adr/0042-continued-fractions.md) | Exact continued fractions on the scratchpad: the convergent recurrence as the first expression over the slot, a rational tolerance test, a bounded coefficient search pinned by an oracle outside the tree; the meet-in-the-middle search Specified |
| [ADR-0043](adr/0043-discovery-path.md) | The discovery path in the runtime: a clause store's description length as the free energy, an invention's drop as the valence and a quarter of it as the reward, a prover frame's payload certifying a node; no executor field, the interoceptive record out of the image |
| [ADR-0044](adr/0044-reference-network.md) | The reference network: a seeded anatomical prior in `cortex-connectome`, the runtime's synthesis, a drive that is a function of the tick and a causal branching-ratio oracle by perturbation of forks with attribution through the connectome; the estimator, the controller and a night's replay measured at 256 and 1 024 units; H-8 and H-9 dispositioned at that scale, Appendix A's a Target |
| [ADR-0045](adr/0045-clause-search.md) | The executive clause search: the candidate pairs and a bounded proof search in `cortex-reasoning`, the runtime's search that commits an invention whose reward is positive and undoes every other; H-11's symbolic half decided, its synaptic half open; the lexicon and the gating circuit not adopted |
| [ADR-0046](adr/0046-lexicon.md) | The lexicon: a caller's table of token ids to concept ids with a lexical shape in the runtime, categories instantiated on the term arena, a token sequence read into a frame with its markers and a frame realised into token ids with its nested clause, markers and particle; a role term that names its filler; `surface_token_id` written; the words the host's |
| [ADR-0047](adr/0047-second-prior-and-the-estimator.md) | A second anatomical prior and the estimator's regime: the sparse random network as the same generator at its widest window; the lag-$k$ slope over a caller's series in `cortex-homeostasis`; the record's estimate cross-checked against the train and the fine-bin slopes measured beside the causal ratio on both priors; no second line of the record |
| [ADR-0048](adr/0048-episodes-tagged-from-the-train.md) | Episodes tagged from the spike train: the densest span and the pattern that fired most in `cortex-hippocampus`, the runtime's composition with the ledger and with a rewarded invention; what a night does to an experience's pattern, the network's own and the invention's at 256 and 1 024 units; H-9's open item and H-11's synaptic half decided at that scale; the online capture Specified |
| [ADR-0049](adr/0049-dale-principle-in-plasticity.md) | Dale's principle in plasticity: a block's polarity is its presynaptic unit's flag, taken by the rules of `cortex-core` as an argument; the eligibility trace is the change of the weight's magnitude and consolidation keeps the weight within the polarity's half of the width; an inhibitory block takes the symmetric window of Vogels et al. 2011 with a depression per presynaptic spike from a stated target rate; F-36 resolved and the nights pinned again |
| [ADR-0050](adr/0050-the-train-inside-the-executor.md) | The spike train inside the executor: every worker's spikes of a tick merged after the tick in unit order into a bounded ring, bit-identical on every worker count; the forks return it; the capture rules and the discovery loop (the search, the reward, the tag, one association per commit) composed over it in one call; the store, the affect state and the association in the image Specified |
| [ADR-0051](adr/0051-the-estimator-at-4096-units.md) | The estimator at 4 096 units: the measurement of ADR-0047 on both priors at the third size as the weekly job's `exhaustive` test, the decision rule for a record line written before the run, and its outcome: no line, since the fine-bin slope reads above the gross causal ratio where the population nears the ceiling |
| [ADR-0052](adr/0052-the-term-arena-in-the-image.md) | The term arena in the image and the discovery loop inside the tick: the clause store, the term nodes, the arena's counters, the affect state and the episode's binding to an invented predicate are the executor's and the image's (format 14); a commit's outputs instantiated through the bindings so the store reads without the table; the search on a cadence of the tick while awake from a cursor it keeps |
| [ADR-0053](adr/0053-the-waking-day-and-the-target-period.md) | The waking day and the inhibitory target period: the period a parameter of the image, not of the registry, whose behaviour gate admits no parameter that changes what the engine does (F-37); the rules taking the depression per spike as an argument; a day measured at 256 and 1 024 units under two periods; the sleep placeholders kept with the reading |
| [ADR-0054](adr/0054-the-causal-count-inside-the-loop.md) | The causal count inside the loop: a message bit, the unit's stamp of a synapse's last message and the descendant rule at the oracle's latency, tallied beside the spikes and measured against the oracle at three sizes on both priors under a rule written first; the count reads the input's density and not $\sigma$, so the record and the controller are untouched; the determinism pin moved for the stamp |
| [ADR-0055](adr/0055-a-weight-that-settles.md) | A weight that settles: the excitatory depression scaled by the weight's magnitude, so that under stationary pairing a magnitude has a fixed point where its depression equals its potentiation; the potentiation additive as it is; measured over the day at 256 and 1 024 units under a criterion written first (the 1 024-unit clause held at both periods, the 256-unit clause at one); §8.8's per-unit gain Specified, since a gain cannot lift a weight from zero; the determinism pin moved for the depression's amount |
| [ADR-0056](adr/0056-a-compaction-of-the-term-arena.md) | A compaction of the term arena: the nodes the clause store does not reach reclaimed by one descending pass that marks and one ascending pass that moves, a rule of `cortex-reasoning` over its own bottom-up arena; the executor runs it between ticks on a call and at every entry into slow-wave sleep; the image unchanged |
| [ADR-0057](adr/0057-the-inhibitory-rule-from-below-the-rail.md) | The inhibitory rule read from below the rail: the reference generator with its inhibitory gain at 2.0, a day at 256 and 1 024 units at the two periods with the fraction of units at the target as the reading a chosen target needs, under a rule written first; no target chosen; no lane for a parameter that changes behaviour, which `CLAUDE.md`'s invariant and ADR-0031 keep with the maintainers |
| [ADR-0058](adr/0058-the-weekly-sweep-and-its-timeouts.md) | The weekly sweep's cost is its timeouts, not its mutants: a mutation run per area with an absolute timeout matched to that area's own tests, a completeness check that refuses a partial sweep, and the runtime's area sharded six ways by the rule that measured it; F-38 resolved |
| [ADR-0059](adr/0059-a-task-a-readout-and-a-reward.md) | A task, a readout and a reward: the runtime composes a two-alternative task on the executor, a stimulus drawn from the trial's index and a seed, a readout that counts a trial's spikes per set from the executor's own train and selects through `cortex-basal-ganglia`'s gating rule, and a reward whose sign is the outcome's; every mis-read and every silent no-op refused by name; the harness's state the caller's and the image format unchanged |
| [ADR-0060](adr/0060-the-reward-path-measured.md) | The reward path measured: the task on the reference network at 256 and 1 024 units under a criterion written before the run, against a shuffled reward, a fixed modulation, the mirrored assignment and a second worker count; the accuracy at chance in every block of every run, so the rewarded clause fails and no constant moves; the reason located in the readout's window and the pairing statistics, not the modulator; what a next round would change named and not built |
| [ADR-0061](adr/0061-the-learning-runs-leave-the-gate.md) | The learning runs leave the gate: brief 027's five full runs at 256 units become weekly `exhaustive` tests and the gate keeps the rewarded run's first block, because a run in the gate is paid again by every runtime mutant of the weekly sweep (amends ADR-0060) |
| [ADR-0062](adr/0062-the-first-complete-sweeps-list.md) | The first complete sweep's list: a triage rule that tells an inherent timeout from a directive-class one, thirty-six timeouts classified and four loops made ranges, twenty-one survivors dispositioned by a test, a shape or a named exclusion, and one timeout that was a slow pass starved by the hang beside it |
| [ADR-0063](adr/0063-the-sweep-reads-its-own-timeouts.md) | The sweep reads its own timeouts: the weekly job writes, beside `timeout.txt`, when each timeout ran, what ran in the shard's other slot and whether its test run was still finishing tests, from the artifact it already keeps; it reports and does not classify, because progress on another thread is not progress toward a verdict (F-42) |
| [ADR-0064](adr/0064-the-documentation-gate-and-the-version.md) | The documentation gate is one command and the whitepaper's version is checked: CI runs `npm run spec` rather than a list of steps that drifted from it (F-44), the document's two version declarations must agree (F-43), and a pull request that changes the document moves its version |
| [ADR-0065](adr/0065-the-instrument-recalibrated.md) | The instrument recalibrated: a set as a periodic pattern of units so that a stimulus is units spaced beyond the prior's local window and fires each once, a readout window derived from the prior's delay band, a trial of one time constant of the dopamine signal, and a calibration with the weights frozen that shows the readout sees the stimulus before any reward is given (1.75 fails and 2.0 passes at 256 units, 1.75 passes first at 1 024); every constant committed before the first rewarded run; format 14 | accepted | 2026-09-20 |
| [ADR-0066](adr/0066-the-reward-path-measured-again.md) | The reward path measured again, through the calibrated instrument: at 256 and 1 024 units under a criterion over the last 128 trials written first, the rewarded run reads 53 and 49 correct against the 80 asked for, the mirrored assignment 53 and 65, the controls hold and the run is the same on one worker and on four; the instrument resolves the stimulus from the background but not one stimulus from the other, every coupling falls alike under every feedback, and at 256 units the resolution is lost within a block once the weights move; no constant moves, and what a next round would change is named | accepted | 2026-09-20 |
| [ADR-0067](adr/0067-the-weekly-dispatch-has-a-scope.md) | The weekly dispatch has a scope: a round runs the exhaustive tests, where its own measurement lives since ADR-0061, and runs the whole-tree sweep beside them only when it added or removed a test, moved one between the gate and the weekly job, or changed the sweep itself; a scheduled run always does both |
| [ADR-0068](adr/0068-the-reward-addressed.md) | The reward addressed: the modulation a synapse consolidates under is a function of both its ends, the dopamine term reaching the synapses from the units of the stimulus presented onto the units of the readout the engine selected and every other synapse consolidating under the baseline alone; two flags per unit and a modulation per slot in the fan-out, the rule, the records and the image unchanged, a run that addresses every unit ADR-0066's bit for bit; written before any rewarded run, what the delivery can do on a two-alternative task with a symmetric reward | accepted | 2026-09-20 |
| [ADR-0069](adr/0069-the-addressed-reward-measured.md) | The addressed reward measured: the instrument, the controls, the seeds and the criterion of ADR-0066 with the delivery of ADR-0068 the one variable; at 1 024 units the addressed rewarded run reads 56 correct of the last 128 and the mirrored assignment 65 against the 80 asked for, the addressed shuffled reward 53, the fixed modulation 60 and the global form 49 (ADR-0066's, reproduced), the run the same on one worker and on four, so the rewarded clause fails with the global form reproduced; the couplings from a stimulus into its rewarded readout part from the other two by three to five per cent in the direction the trace's sign set, and the behaviour reads nothing of it; at 256 units the instrument is below the mark from the first block; no constant moves, and what a next round would change is named | accepted | 2026-09-20 |
| [ADR-0070](adr/0070-where-256-units-settle.md) | Where 256 units settle: the excitatory sum read over eighty windows under the drive alone on the instrument's executor; brief 026's clause first holds at the ninth window at 0.818 of the prior's and the sum is still falling at the eightieth at 0.551, by 0.24 per cent per window, so the clause reads a rate and the item is discharged with both numbers; behind the lead-in of nine windows the rule derived, the calibration's measure reads 50 of 64 in the first block and 34 to 44 after, so 256 units is not usable for this task behind a lead-in of any length and a learning round's criterion stays at 1 024 units; no constant moves | accepted | 2026-09-21 |
| [ADR-0071](adr/0071-the-exhaustive-job-times-its-own-binaries.md) | The whole-domain tests report what each binary took: one duration for thirty-seven `exhaustive` tests across four binaries could not say what a round had added — seven runs read 32 to 71 minutes while about twenty-one heavy runs were added, and two runs of identical content read 40 and 71 — so the job reads the seconds cargo already prints per binary into the summary and an artifact, and sharding it stays a decision for the round that has the numbers (F-45) |
| [ADR-0072](adr/0072-what-the-trace-is-made-of.md) | What the trace is made of: the eligibility on a stimulus's synapses into a readout, read from the record at 1 024 units with the weights frozen and split by what paired it through an oracle that replays the pair rule over the executor's own train and agrees with the record at every reading; the volley's pairing is net potentiation and the background's net depression of four to five times it, so ADR-0066's sentence is a measurement, and its mechanism is the count of a stimulus unit's spikes within the pair window after its volley (F-46); a ladder of gains below the present one passes at no rung under the sight and the sign, so there is no rewarded run and no constant moves |
| [ADR-0073](adr/0073-the-whole-domain-tests-sharded.md) | The whole-domain tests run in three shards: the first per-binary table showed one job at 1 h 43 m of its 120-minute bound with half of it in `instrument.rs`, so the shards are taken from the binaries `--list` names at run time rather than from a list written down, each checks what it ran against what the list counted, and the floor — the largest single binary — is named rather than hidden |
| [ADR-0074](adr/0074-a-stimulus-that-fires-once.md) | A stimulus that fires each unit once, the one change F-46 names, tried as one message at the unit's threshold, one of 1.25 and one of 1.125, each read over a frozen run at 1 024 units and the gain 1.75 by a measure written first that cannot see the answer; the executor scales an injected message by the synaptic gain (F-47), so F-46's shape fires a unit at rest twice and one message fires it once or not at all but never every unit once under the drive's standing potential; no candidate passes, the trace stays net depression under each and ADR-0072's hypothesis of +5 is not met, so there is no rewarded run and no constant moves; what the criterion requires is read off the trials for the first time: one to three spikes per trial on the answer readout, the size the delivery moves and in the wrong direction |
| [ADR-0075](adr/0075-the-dispatch-scope-follows-the-diff.md) | The dispatch's scope follows the diff: a round sweeps the whole tree only when its actual change can have left a mutant uncaught — source under `src/`, a test removed or moved out of the swept suite, the sweep's own configuration — decided by the executing round from its diff rather than written into the brief before the diff exists; adding a test never qualifies, because an added test can only catch more, and three of the four measuring rounds since ADR-0067 swept a tree they had not touched |
| [ADR-0076](adr/0076-two-injections.md) | Two injections: F-46's drive kept whole and a cancel, a negative basal message into the same units inside the trial, as a field of the task's stimulus with its refusals and a bit-for-bit pin; the brief's offset lands inside the refractory window, which the membrane rule drops, and its sizes are too small for the soma the coupling primed, so the cancel is derived from the rule — the tick after the window, over the nine ticks the volley spreads, six messages at the bound per tick — and held to the engine's probe; under it every unit fires once and not again, the volley's depression goes to nothing and the background's halves, the volley's potentiation halves too because the response measured before was to three volleys, and the terms sum to −2.0 per synapse per trial against the +5 hypothesised: the sight passes, the sign reads 13 of 64, there is no rewarded run, and H-12's stopping rule records the stimulus side as exhausted |
| [ADR-0077](adr/0077-the-background-side.md) | The background side, step 2 of H-12's stopping rule: the network settled before the task under the gain held (ADR-0055's criterion as an integer rule, held at the ninety-sixth window at 0.925 of the prior's with the readout units' rate the prior's) and the criticality controller on at its step (settled at the thirty-fifth at 0.453, the gain swinging between 2.2 and 2.8 and the readouts seven to twenty-four times louder, as predicted), each alone, run quiet, saved as an image with the baseline at zero and read over a frozen run by three measures written first; the settled network fires once, is seen at 62 of 64 and its trace is net potentiation on average for the first time at a gain at which the readout sees (+0.4 per synapse per trial), but the sign reads 53 against 56; the controller fails all three; no candidate passes, there is no rewarded run, and by the rule's step 3 H-12 closes as a no for this task in this regime, the three routes the next decision chooses among named and not built |
| [ADR-0078](adr/0078-the-rewards-direction.md) | After H-12, the reward's direction before a task's difference: of the three routes H-12's stopping rule named, the task that asks for a sign is chosen and written as H-13 — on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the baseline at zero, a reward delivered after every trial to the synapses from the presented stimulus onto its assigned readout raises those couplings and the response the engine reads from that readout, against the same trials with the reward withheld (a sign test on paired trials, 80 of the last 128, in both assignments) — with its criterion, its prediction and its own stopping rule written before any run; the structural rule deferred for want of a measured need; the operating regime recorded as the open question a no on H-13 makes the next decision |
| [ADR-0079](adr/0079-the-rewards-direction-measured.md) | The reward's direction, measured: H-13 is yes — on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the baseline at zero, a reward delivered after every trial to the synapses from the presented stimulus onto its assigned readout raised those couplings by 12 to 15 per cent over 512 trials, still rising, and that readout's response above the same trial's with the reward withheld in 128 and 127 trials of the last 128, by six spikes per trial, in both assignments, every synapse outside the pairs bit for bit the image's; the calibration reproduced ADR-0077 first, the oracle replayed the consolidation and agreed with the record at every trial, the trace's potentiation grew as the couplings rose and the two mechanisms named as bounding it were outrun; the next decision is an ADR on the reinforced form, not taken there |
| [ADR-0080](adr/0080-the-reinforced-form.md) | The reinforced form, H-14 written before any run: on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the baseline at zero, the task as ADR-0059 and ADR-0068 built it — the reward addressed to the synapses from the presented stimulus onto the readout the engine selected, its sign the outcome's — asked to move the selection to the answer, 80 of the last 128 in the assignment and the mirrored assignment, over 1 536 trials, a length derived from H-13's blocks before any run; a wrong selection's pair shown by the rules never to consolidate, the shuffled reward read for lock-in rather than bounded by a clause, the relation to H-12 and the stopping rule written first |
| [ADR-0081](adr/0081-the-reinforced-form-measured.md) | The reinforced form, measured: H-14 is yes — on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the baseline at zero, the task as built moves the selection to the answer in 128 of the last 128 trials in the assignment and in the mirrored assignment over 1 536 trials, the selection passing 40 of 64 per block by trial 448 and 384, the mirrored first, as predicted and earlier than estimated; the answer couplings rise to 1.35 to 1.45 of the image's and are still rising, every synapse outside them — the wrong pairs among them — bit for bit the image's, ADR-0080's derivation true on every arm; the shuffled reward locks one stimulus onto one readout; the calibration reproduced ADR-0077 first; H-14's stopping rule at step 3, the note written beside H-12 and the next decision named and not taken |
| [ADR-0082](adr/0082-plasticity-everywhere.md) | Plasticity everywhere, H-15 written before any run: H-14's configuration with the modulation baseline at 0.5 instead of zero — brief 027's, the baseline ADR-0077's lead-in settled under and H-12's rewarded runs used — every other constant H-14's; 80 correct of the last 128 in both assignments; the drift the unrewarded consolidation brings predicted from ADR-0077's terms to favour readout 1 and read in a withheld arm; the note beside H-12 for a yes and the stopping rule written first; the runs in a test binary of their own, named so that the round robin leaves `instrument.rs` alone in its shard |
| [ADR-0083](adr/0083-plasticity-everywhere-measured.md) | Plasticity everywhere, measured: H-15 is no — on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the modulation baseline at 0.5, the task as built moves the selection to the answer in 106 of the last 128 trials in the mirrored assignment and in 78 in the assignment, two short of the mark; the unrewarded consolidation carries every coupling up by a third to a half of the image's and faster onto readout 1, the withheld arm drifting there for both stimuli as predicted, the reward parting an answer pair from that course by two to nine per cent, the stimulus whose answer is readout 0 deciding each arm as predicted; the assertion true on every arm, the calibration reproduced ADR-0077 first, the inhibitory sum at 0.41 of the image's after the run; H-15's stopping rule at step 4, nothing beside H-12, the next decision named and not taken; the harness shared as one module between `instrument.rs` and a binary of its own |
| [ADR-0084](adr/0084-the-shards-take-tests.md) | The shards take tests, not binaries: `instrument.rs` read 61 per cent of its shard's bound with nothing added to it, so every binary's tests are spread over the three shards, each binary's share run in one process with its tests as exact filters read from `--list` at run time; a listing that fails or names nothing now fails the shard (F-48); ADR-0082's rule on a binary's name superseded |
| [ADR-0085](adr/0085-inhibition-off-the-reward-gate.md) | Inhibition off the reward's gate: of H-15's two routes, consolidation gated by the reward is chosen and its named cost taken first — an inhibitory synapse may consolidate under a baseline of its own, a parameter of the image (format 15), while every excitatory synapse stays under the reward's gate; written as H-16 before any run, H-14's configuration with that inhibitory baseline at 0.5, which splits H-15's no into its excitatory and inhibitory parts; the reward-prediction error and the regime named for after it |
| [ADR-0086](adr/0086-the-inhibitory-baseline-built.md) | The inhibitory baseline built: ADR-0085's parameter in the modulator section's reserved bytes — a flag byte at `[24]` and a Q16.16 value at `[28..32)` — the image format moved from 14 to 15, unset encoded as the zeros a format-14 writer left, the configuration taking an `Option`, a third modulation published beside ADR-0068's two and picked by the block's polarity in the fan-out; unset, every pinned number and the determinism pin stand bit for bit |
| [ADR-0087](adr/0087-inhibition-off-the-reward-gate-measured.md) | Inhibition off the reward's gate, measured: H-16 is yes — on the network ADR-0077 settled, with the modulation baseline at zero and the inhibitory baseline at 0.5, the task as built moves the selection to the answer in 128 of the last 128 trials in both assignments, the crossing at trials 512 and 256 beside H-14's 448 and 384, while the inhibitory sum falls in every block to 0.41 of the image's as under H-15 and every excitatory synapse outside the two answer pairs ends the run the image's bit for bit; H-15's no put on the excitatory side; the withheld arm's lean a coin either way, so ADR-0085's second predicted reading did not hold as written for A; the calibration reproduced ADR-0077 first; H-16's stopping rule at step 3, the configuration the engine learns in named and the next decision named and not taken |
| [ADR-0088](adr/0088-a-fourth-shard.md) | A fourth shard: the three shards read 48, 23 and 77 per cent of their bound on a runner two and a half times slower than the one before it, and every measuring round adds a twenty-minute test, so the matrix takes a fourth shard and the script its `n`; the floor is now the longest single test, which is said rather than hidden |
| [ADR-0089](adr/0089-the-assignment-reversed.md) | The assignment reversed: of the candidates H-16's stopping rule named, the reversal is chosen and written as H-17 before any run — H-16's configuration over 3 072 trials with the answer's mapping flipped at the half, in both directions, at least 80 correct of the last 128 before the flip and after it; predicted **no**, because the gate is a deterministic comparison with no exploration and under the reward's gate a pair consolidates only when it is selected and correct, so a learned engine selects the old answer and earns nothing; the round measures how seldom it selects the other, which is the measured need the mechanism after it must meet |
| [ADR-0090](adr/0090-the-old-answer-held-from-the-trial-after-the-flip.md) | The old answer held from the trial after the flip: ADR-0089's assertion amended before any run — a trial's delivery is consolidated in the trial after it, so the first trial under the second mapping still consolidates the last reward of the first; the old answer's pairs are held from the end of the 1 537th trial, the other stimulus's old pair from the end of the 1 536th, and every excitatory synapse outside the four stimulus–readout pairs at the image's; nothing else of H-17 changes |
| [ADR-0091](adr/0091-the-assignment-reversed-measured.md) | The assignment reversed, measured: H-17 is no — an engine that learned the answer in 128 of the last 128 trials before the flip selects the new answer in 0 of the last 128 after it, in both arms; over the 1 536 trials after the flip it selects the other readout 2 and 1 times and earns 2 and 1 rewards, the counts' gap at 16 to 24 spikes a trial and the old answer's pairs unmoved through 1 534 punishments each; ADR-0090's assertion held, the first half reproduced H-16 bit for bit; H-17's stopping rule at step 4, the next decision — an exploration in the selection or a depression under the gate — named with these readings as its need and not taken |
| [ADR-0092](adr/0092-the-shards-dealt-by-cost.md) | The shards dealt by cost: the round robin reads names, not costs, and put two of the longest tests in one shard at 66 per cent of its bound, so the tests go longest first to the least-loaded shard, costed by a table the weekly run itself measures — each test in a process of its own, its seconds its own; the table decides the balance and never the coverage, and a line that names no test fails `npm run spec` |
| [ADR-0093](adr/0093-the-punished-pair-consolidates-against-its-trace.md) | The punished pair consolidates against its trace: of the two mechanisms H-17's stopping rule named, the depression under the gate is chosen — a signed gate, a parameter of the image, under which an addressed excitatory synapse consolidates under clamp(baseline + dopamine, −1, 1), so that a punishment moves its weight against its trace's sign and spends the trace by as much; written as H-18 before any run, H-17's configuration with the signed gate set and a second mapping twice as long, 4 608 trials derived from ADR-0091's readings; no prediction for the verdict; the exploration named for after it |
| [ADR-0094](adr/0094-the-signed-gate-built.md) | The signed gate built: ADR-0093's parameter as a flag byte at `[25]` of the modulator section, the image format moved from 15 to 16; `cortex-neuromod`'s `signed_modulation` (the sum clamped to [−1, 1]) beside `modulation`, `cortex-core`'s `consolidate_signed` beside `consolidate`, which is untouched — below zero the weight against the trace's sign, the trace spent by what the magnitude moved; the addressed word the coordinator already publishes carries the signed value while the gate is set, an inhibitory slot taking it at no less than zero; unset, every run the run it was, bit for bit |
| [ADR-0095](adr/0095-an-image-pin-moves-with-its-format.md) | An image's pin moves with its format: ADR-0093's stopping rule amended before any run — the pinned numbers that stop the round when they move with the signed gate unset are the engine's and the records', not the header's version and the seal over it, which ADR-0093 itself orders moved; H-17's whole-image CRC re-pinned at format 16, and the image with its header written back to 15 held to the CRC H-17 read, which CRC-64's linearity showed before the run; nothing else of H-18 changes |
| [ADR-0096](adr/0096-the-punished-pair-measured.md) | The punished pair, measured: H-18 is yes — on H-17's configuration with the signed gate set, 128 of the last 128 before the flip and 128 of the last 128 at the 4 608th trial in both arms; the old answer's pairs, punished against their traces at 9 070 to 13 310 a punished trial at first, fell back to 0.95 to 1.06 of the image's and each stimulus's selection crossed in the fourteenth to the twentieth block after the flip; a punishment potentiated 18 to 24 per cent of the addressed synapse-trials it reached and depressed slightly more; ADR-0093's three predicted readings held; the assertion held; the calibration reproduced ADR-0077; the inhibitory sum stopped falling at 0.11 of the image's; H-18's stopping rule at step 3, the configuration the engine learns and revises in named, the next decision named and not taken |
| [ADR-0097](adr/0097-the-active-set-measured.md) | The active set, measured: the executor counts its turns, held on every tick to the units whose gate is scheduled; one message of 0.125 keeps an armed unit awake 2 210 ticks at the gain 1.0 and 2 503 at 1.75, an oracle of the membrane's rule and the engine agreeing; under ADR-0044's drive 99.99 per cent of the reference network's units are served on every tick at 1 024 and 4 096 units while 0.0018 per cent fire, and at drives sixteen and 256 times sparser, where it never fires, 72.0 and 7.38 per cent against the floor $1 - e^{-rD}$ written first, 70.54 and 7.35; the budget Appendix A's three targets must fit written for per-tick service and for service on arrival — per tick, about 26 000 units in real time on 64 workers at ADR-0044's drive, or 43 million about 1 700 times slower, at an estimated 25 ns a turn — and F-49; no integration model chosen |
| [ADR-0098](adr/0098-the-integration-model.md) | The integration model: every awake unit integrated every tick, as built, since the maintainers prefer it and ADR-0097's budget shows that service on arrival buys nothing without a catch-up the membrane does not have; Appendix A's three targets met in two modes — a real-time mode, with a body attached, whose unit count is what the budget allows at the input's density (a Target of about 2.6 × 10⁴ units on 64 workers at ADR-0044's), and an offline mode, with none, whose unit count is what memory holds and whose run is slower than real time; F-49 resolved; five levers on the engine's speed named, and the three that change no rule (the lookahead, a sweep without the gate, a working layout for the vector units) taken first, before the learning line resumes |
| [ADR-0099](adr/0099-the-engines-speed.md) | The engine's speed: the three levers that change no rule, in the order the sizes the tree runs can read them — the sweep without the gate first, a working layout of the integrated fields for the vector units second, the lookahead third; each held to every reading of behaviour bit for bit, a pin that also holds the scheduler's own bytes restated only when shown equal with those bytes masked, and each kept only if a gain written before its first timed run is read (at most 0.80 of the base's wall time per tick at ADR-0097's runs (a) and (d), at most 1.10 at (b) and (c)); axiom A3's enforcement opened to the sweep's round; brief 043 builds the first |
| [ADR-0100](adr/0100-the-sweep-without-the-gate.md) | The sweep without the gate, built and not kept: each worker owning a fixed range of the unit arena and serving it in unit order from a schedule bitmap, with no deque, no stealing and no compare-and-swap in the turn, held every reading of behaviour bit for bit and ran 2.0 to 2.7 times faster wherever most units are served, and 1.18 times slower at ADR-0097's sparsest drive; under ADR-0099's criterion, written before the first timed run, it is not kept; two diagnostics beside the criterion place the regression in the workload's census of every unit between ticks; the code stays in the history, and the next decision is named and not taken |
| [ADR-0101](adr/0101-the-sweep-measured-again.md) | The sweep measured again: of ADR-0100's named options the second is taken — the sweep without the gate, as ADR-0100 built it, timed again on ADR-0097's four runs with no census in the timed ticks, against ADR-0099's bounds unchanged; the workload chosen after the first reading failed, which the record says; the census kept in the tests of behaviour; a lever's gain read on a workload whose instrument reads no record the engine does not; F-50 resolved; brief 044 |
| [ADR-0102](adr/0102-the-sweep-timed-with-no-census.md) | The sweep timed with no census, and kept: ADR-0100's sweep without the gate, re-applied as built by reverting its revert, read 0.517, 0.527, 0.745 and 0.643 of the base's wall time per tick at ADR-0097's runs (a) to (d) on ADR-0101's workload, inside every bound of ADR-0099, with every reading of behaviour held bit for bit; ADR-0099's census workload read again beside it at 1.156 at (c); the census-free path, the mutation gate and the protocol written before the first timed run; axiom A3 enforced by ownership, ADR-0017's and ADR-0023's decisions amended; brief 044 |
| [ADR-0103](adr/0103-the-working-layout.md) | The working layout: ADR-0099's second lever — phase 1 may gather a chunk of a worker's scheduled units' integrated fields into lanes, integrate them together and write them back within the phase, or run `integrate`'s arithmetic in lanes within one record; the 64-byte record stays the only home of a unit's state across ticks, so quality goal 2 holds unchanged (ADR-0001's scratch); safe Rust for the compiler's vectorizer as ADR-0039's hypervector, no intrinsic, no target feature, no dependency; `integrate` unchanged and every lane held to it by a property test over the lattice; the gain on ADR-0101's workload against ADR-0099's bounds; a persistent working copy left to an ADR of its own; brief 045 |
| [ADR-0104](adr/0104-the-membrane-in-lanes.md) | The membrane's rule in lanes, built and not kept: ADR-0103's first option — a chunk of eight scheduled units in two vectors of four `i32` lanes, every step at one width (`integrate`'s two `i64` steps rewritten in `i32`, the inputs read in every lane and masked), every lane held to `integrate` over the lattice, vectorized by the compiler at the baseline, the mutation gate 63 of 63 before the run — read 1.209, 1.132, 1.211 and 1.122 of the base's wall time per tick at ADR-0097's runs (a) to (d) against ADR-0099's bounds, as predicted before the run: moving the fields through the lanes cost more than the vector rule saved against a scalar rule whose branches the reference network makes predictable; the code reverted and in the history; F-51, the integration about two thirds of a turn and not all of it, resolved by `neuron/integrate_x1024`; the next decision named and not taken; brief 045 |
| [ADR-0105](adr/0105-the-speed-line-closed.md) | The speed line closed: of ADR-0099's levers the sweep without the gate is kept (ADR-0102) and the membrane's rule in lanes is not (ADR-0104); the reference network at 1 024 units on two workers now runs faster than a tick of real time on a developer machine; what is left — the lookahead, the rest of the turn, a persistent working copy, a wider baseline, the two rule changes — named with the need that would take each; the learning line resumes (ADR-0106) |
| [ADR-0106](adr/0106-the-reward-prediction-error.md) | The reward-prediction error: of the four questions H-18's stopping rule named, the reward-prediction error is taken — the task keeps an expected reward per stimulus, the dopamine signal receives the outcome's reward less that expectation, which then moves by a thirty-second of the error; written as H-19 before any run on H-18's configuration, schedule and arms: does the engine learn, revise and settle, where H-18's couplings never stopped rising; the tension between settling and revising stated before the run; no prediction for the verdict; brief 046 |
| [ADR-0107](adr/0107-the-critic-built.md) | The critic built: ADR-0106's expected reward per stimulus placed in the task as `Task::critic`, off unless set — the reward delivered the outcome's less the presented stimulus's expectation, the expectation then moved by an arithmetic shift of the error, `Outcome` carrying the error and the expectation before and after, an expectation beyond the reward's magnitude refused, no record field and no format, and unset every run the run it was; the harness's critic written a second time; H-19's constants, rules and gate before any rewarded run; the calibration extended to H-18's first block; F-52 (three of H-18's moves mis-rounded in ADR-0106) and F-53 (ADR-0106's arithmetic of the fading without the dopamine signal's carry-over); brief 046 |
| [ADR-0108](adr/0108-the-reward-prediction-error-measured.md) | The reward-prediction error, measured: H-19 is no, clause 3 the one that failed — with the critic set the engine learned the mapping (122 and 125 of the last 128 before the flip) and revised it (119 and 121 of the last 128 at the 4 608th trial) in both arms, and over each mapping's last 256 trials one answer pair of each mapping moved by 1.22 to 1.87 per cent of its image coupling and the other by −0.08 to +0.34, where H-18's moved by 3.6 to 9.5; the expectations about a tenth below the reward because the selection stayed wrong or tied in two to five trials of 64; ADR-0106's two predicted readings held; the assertion held; the calibration reproduced ADR-0077 and H-18's first block; H-19's stopping rule at step 4, and the next decision — what else keeps the couplings rising — named and not taken; brief 046 |
| [ADR-0109](adr/0109-a-schedule-of-reversals.md) | A schedule of reversals: H-19's stopping rule at step 4 on clause 3 alone — what keeps the couplings rising is ADR-0108's two readings, the advantage the critic delivers while the selection still errs and an error's carry-over; an expectation per stimulus and readout, which would remove the first by delivering at most 32 full rewards to a pair in a whole run and so starve the learning, rejected; H-19's configuration kept as the one the engine learns and revises in, and H-18's second named question taken under it: written as H-20 before any run, H-19's arms and critic through three reversals over 7 680 trials, the first 56 blocks H-19's bit for bit, yes when every mapping is learned and every coupling stays at or below 1.30 of its image's; the speed of each reversal read beside it; no prediction for the verdict; brief 047 |
| [ADR-0110](adr/0110-a-schedule-of-reversals-measured.md) | A schedule of reversals, measured: H-20 is yes — through three reversals over 7 680 trials the engine learned every mapping, 113 to 125 of each mapping's last 128 trials in both arms, and no stimulus–readout coupling passed 1.30 of its image's at any block's end, the highest 1.166 and 1.174; the first 56 blocks H-19's bit for bit; each reversal 13 to 23 blocks to 40 of 64, no steady speed-up; the answer pairs still rising at every flip, by up to 4.9 per cent over a mapping's last 256 trials; the inhibitory sum level at about 0.07 and 0.08 of the image's from the 111th block; both predicted readings held; the assertion held; H-20's stopping rule at step 3, and the next decision named and not taken; brief 047 |
| [ADR-0111](adr/0111-a-rule-held-by-the-network.md) | A rule held by the network: beside the four questions H-20's stopping rule named, the maintainers add a representation of the rule in force — a context held by the network's own activity, switched by an error, under which each mapping is kept apart — with H-20's reversal speeds (no learning set) as its need, and take it in three rounds: first, measured and nothing changed, whether an assembly of the settled network holds its activity after a kick, stays quiet without one and lets go on a signal, the rules giving reasons to doubt it (short-term plasticity depression-dominated, the threshold adapting); then a readout gated by the context; then the schedule of reversals asked again with it; brief 048 measures the first |
| [ADR-0112](adr/0112-an-assembly-that-holds.md) | An assembly that holds, measured: ADR-0111's first round, nothing of the engine changed — on ADR-0077's settled image with every weight frozen, assemblies of 16, 32 and 64 excitatory units wired as the test's own network at a quarter, a half, three quarters and the top of Q1.15's range, run through a lead-in and eight rounds of an unkicked, a kicked and a released epoch under the reference drive, the kick a ramp with a reset derived from the membrane rule, the release ADR-0076's cancel, the rules and the arithmetic written before any run; no cell is usable: three hold by the rule and each also ignites without a kick and does not let go, and the holds are population bursts that drain the members' vesicle pool to a few per cent, never a sustained rate; F-54; the next decision, an ADR on a mechanism of persistence, named and not taken |
| [ADR-0113](adr/0113-a-facilitating-class-of-synapses.md) | A facilitating class of synapses: ADR-0112's branch for nothing usable taken, the facilitating route chosen over a slower current — a unit may be marked facilitating, its synapses releasing under the Tsodyks–Markram rule at the class's own constants, a parameter of the image, unset bit for bit; by an integer replica of `step_stp`, ADR-0019's constants leave a kicked assembly weaker than an unkicked one for about four epochs after its burst and never primed, while a release fraction of 0.2 or 0.1 with facilitation over 2^16 ticks (the longest the factor resolves) and depression over 2^13 primes it from under a tenth of a second to about a second after its burst, at up to 1.38 or 1.60 times the unkicked release; brief 049 builds the class and measures ADR-0112's assemblies again with their members marked, over spans that tell a refreshed hold from a fading one |
| [ADR-0114](adr/0114-the-facilitating-class-built.md) | The facilitating class built: ADR-0113's mark as bit 2 of a unit's `flags` (`FLAG_FACILITATING`), which `integrate` leaves as it finds it; the class's $U$ and two shifts a parameter of the image behind a flag byte at `[32..36)` of the modulator section, refused outside what the rule resolves, a mark refused while no class is set, and the image format moved from 16 to 17; `step_stp_class` beside `step_stp`, which is untouched and which it is bit for bit at ADR-0019's constants; each worker holding the class from `Executor::new`, so that the tick reads no new word; unset, every run the run it was, and the two whole-image pins re-pinned with the format as ADR-0095 did; brief 049 |
| [ADR-0115](adr/0115-the-assemblies-marked.md) | The assemblies marked, measured: ADR-0112's assemblies of 16, 32 and 64 units at six recurrent weights under each of ADR-0113's two sets, every member marked and every weight frozen, through a lead-in and eight rounds of an unkicked span, a hold span with ADR-0112's kick, a release derived from the class and a tail, by rules written before any run; the arithmetic with the class's own step agreeing with ADR-0113's tables but for one sentence of its prose (F-55); one cell usable, 64 units under set (ii) at a quarter of the range, holding as a train of bursts refreshed inside the class's primed window, quiet unkicked and letting go when released, while every other cell never holds or runs away; the next decision, a readout gated by the context, named and not taken; brief 049 |
| [ADR-0116](adr/0116-the-region-before-the-readout.md) | The region before the readout: ADR-0115's next decision deferred by one round — before a readout is gated by the context, the usable region is measured for its width and for whether it survives another draw of the delays, a network drained as H-20's arm leaves it and the task's own stimulus, on the geometry the task and the context will share; F-54 decided: the readouts give up places 5 and 16, eight places each, so that an assembly of up to 102 units at ADR-0112's placement is in no set, beside the task's geometry, which every earlier test keeps; a cell robust when usable in the core grid and under each condition, the second round's cell the robust one with the most usable neighbours; brief 050 measures, nothing of the engine changed |
| [ADR-0117](adr/0117-the-region-measured.md) | The region measured: brief 050, nothing of the engine changed — ADR-0115's marked assemblies under set (ii) at 48 to 96 units and five weights from 0.125 to 0.375 of Q1.15's range on the settled network, and a subset of six cells under another draw of the delays, on the image H-20's arm leaves frozen and with the task's stimulus every epoch, on the geometry F-54's decision gives the context, by ADR-0115's protocol and rules for a robust cell written first; the usable region one weight, a quarter of the range, at 64, 80 and 96 units; no cell robust — the stimulus and the drained network each tip the usable cells into igniting, and 96 units fail under the other seed — so no cell for the second round; a held context reaches both readouts alike; the eight-place readouts still resolve the stimulus; the drained image's dopamine signal put at rest (F-56); the next decision named and not taken |
| [ADR-0118](adr/0118-a-fifth-shard.md) | A fifth shard: the weekly job's whole-domain tests dealt to five shards where ADR-0088 dealt them to four, by ADR-0092's deal unchanged — each round's new tests are costed at the table's default of 900 s until their own dispatch measures them, and ADR-0117's first dispatch put one shard at 59 per cent of its bound where the regenerated table replays at 45; at five the present table plans about 36 per cent, and a round the size of brief 050's about 46; one matrix entry and one argument, the script unchanged, no required check renamed |
| [ADR-0119](adr/0119-an-inhibition-of-the-contexts-own.md) | An inhibition of the context's own: among the branches ADR-0117 named for a region no condition leaves robust, the maintainers take the context's own inhibition — every one of the 64 marked members receives sixteen added inhibitory synapses from the network's inhibitory units drawn over the ring, so that the inhibition it meets rises when the network it sits in does, and its recurrent weight is raised against it; the one branch aimed at both the drained network and the task's stimulus, which each tipped the usable cells into igniting; brief 051 measures recurrent weights of 0.25 to 0.75 against added inhibitory weights of −0.25 to −1.0 under ADR-0117's four conditions and rules, nothing of the engine changed |
| [ADR-0120](adr/0120-the-contexts-own-inhibition-measured.md) | The context's own inhibition measured: brief 051, nothing of the engine changed — each of ADR-0117's 64 marked members given sixteen added inhibitory synapses from sixteen of the prior's inhibitory units drawn over the ring, frozen, and the assembly measured at recurrent weights of 0.25 to 0.75 against inhibitory weights of −0.25, −0.5 and −1.0 under ADR-0117's four conditions, ADR-0117's cell reproduced bit for bit under each first, by ADR-0117's rules written first; no cell of the core grid usable — at a quarter of the range nothing holds, from three eighths every cell ignites without a kick — so no cell robust and no cell for the second round; the inhibition lowers the members' background by 10 to 26 per cent and moves the assembly's threshold rather than widening the gap between holding and igniting; its sources fire 1.4 to 2.0 times faster while the assembly bursts; the next decision named and not taken | accepted |
| [ADR-0121](adr/0121-a-sixth-shard.md) | A sixth shard: the weekly job's whole-domain tests dealt to six shards where ADR-0118 dealt them to five, because the table ADR-0120's dispatch regenerated, read on runners about a fifth slower, plans every shard at 57 per cent of its bound at five, and about 47 at six; one matrix entry and one argument, the script unchanged, no required check renamed; the last shard the lever buys, since the longest test alone is 37 per cent |
| [ADR-0122](adr/0122-a-slow-voltage-gated-current.md) | A slow, voltage-gated current: after four rounds that read one bottleneck — a held assembly and a quiet one differ by the release their synapses make per spike, at most about 1.4 to 1.6 times, and every change tried acted on both states alike — the maintainers take ADR-0111's other candidate for persistence: a unit may be marked to carry a slow potential that integrates its excitatory synaptic input over tens of milliseconds, the drive and every injected message excluded, and reaches the soma only as far as the soma is depolarised, so that its contrast follows the members' rate ratio and the quiet state's background input barely reaches the soma (Jahr and Stevens 1990; Lisman, Fellous and Wang 1998; Wang 1999); its state in the record's reserved bytes, its constants a parameter of the image, its rule beside `integrate`, which is untouched, format 18, unset bit for bit; brief 052 builds it and measures ADR-0117's assembly with its members marked, alone and with the facilitating class, under ADR-0117's conditions and rules |
| [ADR-0123](adr/0123-the-slow-current-built.md) | The slow current built: ADR-0122's mark as bit 3 of a unit's `flags` (`FLAG_SLOW`), which `integrate` and its sibling leave as they find it; the slow potential `v_slow` in the record's `[20..24)`, reserved since format 4, zero in every unmarked unit and refused in an image otherwise; the constants — a leak shift from 1 to 16, an input shift from 0 to 16, and the gate's two voltages $0 < V_{lo} < V_{hi} \le 1.0$ — a parameter of the image at the modulator section's `[36..48)`, refused outside what the rule resolves, a mark refused while none is set, format 18; `integrate_slow` beside the untouched `integrate`, which it is bit for bit with no slow potential and no slow input, held there over the lattice and to an `i64` oracle of ADR-0122's text; each worker holding the constants, an unmarked unit's turn gaining the test of its mark and nothing else; unset, every run is the run it was, and the four whole-image pins re-pinned with the format under a masked check |
| [ADR-0124](adr/0124-the-slow-current-measured.md) | The slow current measured: brief 052, the slow current the one rule changed (ADR-0123) — ADR-0117's assembly of 64 members marked for it, (A) under ADR-0019's short-term plasticity and (B) under set (ii) as well, input shifts 0, 1 and 2 placed by a rule written before an arithmetic of the slow rule itself, by ADR-0117's substrate, protocol, measures and rules, every weight frozen; the critical slow potential 1.138 of the threshold, and the slow potential a staircase (F-57); the backgrounds read first: fed by the prior's synapses alone the slow current raises the unwired members' rate from 1.6 Hz to 4 to 54 Hz, because its gate at the drive's mean standing opens on every upward excursion and shrinks the distance a fluctuation must cover to the threshold by the ratio of the slow potential to its critical level; the kick's volley full at every arm and shift and its second clause failed at every one, at shifts 0 and 1 by the members' background alone, so no cell ran — the maintainers chose to stop there rather than move the measure; ADR-0117's cell reproduced; the next decision named and not taken |
| [ADR-0125](adr/0125-the-gate-raised.md) | The gate raised, and a stopping rule for the line: the maintainers take the first of ADR-0124's branches as the last try of the rule held by the network — the slow current's lower gate voltage raised to 0.7, 0.8 and 0.9 of the threshold, a parameter of the image, so nothing of the engine changes — measured on the backgrounds and controls at every arm, shift and voltage and on the core grid only where the kick passes, the conditions left to a round of their own for the weekly job's budget; and, written before any run, the rule that pauses the line: no kick that passes, no usable cell in the core, or no robust cell under the conditions, and the rule representation stops, what was built staying unset bit for bit; brief 053 |
| [ADR-0126](adr/0126-the-gate-raised-measured.md) | The gate raised, measured: brief 053 — ADR-0123's slow current with its lower gate voltage at 0.7, 0.8 and 0.9 of the threshold on ADR-0124's substrate, arms, shifts, protocol, measures and kick, every weight frozen, the protocol, an arithmetic of the slow rule itself and three predicted readings written before any run; the quiet soma, read on both arms' substrates (ADR-0117's background bit for bit, F-58), stands above 0.7, 0.8 and 0.9 of the threshold on 6.0, 1.8 and 0.41 per cent of its ticks; the gate raised lowers every background, to 2.9 to 5.4 Hz at 0.9, and the kick passes at shift 2 at 0.8 and 0.9 under both arms; all sixteen cells there fail, fifteen igniting without a kick and none letting go, a wired assembly's own slow potential making the voltage its members' threshold; ADR-0125's case 2: the line pauses, the facilitating class and the slow current staying unset bit for bit, the next decision (H-20's four open questions or the rule held in the symbolic layer) named and not taken |
| [ADR-0127](adr/0127-the-paused-line-leaves-the-weekly.md) | The paused line leaves the weekly: the rule held by the network paused (ADR-0126), forty of its forty-four whole-domain tests leave the weekly job, renamed from `…_exhaustive` to `…_paused` and kept in the tree with every pin, runnable by `-- --ignored paused`; four stay, the substrate and one for each mechanism the line built (the facilitating class through ADR-0117's cell, the slow current alone and with the class in a wired assembly); the cost table loses the forty lines, 71 tests and 20 405 s, and the weekly job's plan falls from about 61 to about 24 per cent of its bound, nothing of the engine or of any reading changed |
| [ADR-0128](adr/0128-a-target-the-network-fires-at.md) | A target the network fires at: with the rule held by the network paused, the learning line takes the inhibitory drain among H-20's four open questions — under H-20's configuration the inhibitory sum falls to 0.07 and 0.08 of the settled image's, and the inhibitory rule's target rate, a parameter of the image, is its default of 5 Hz while the settled network fires at about 1.7, so from the rail the rule weakens the inhibition onto nearly every unit; the target is set to the settled network's own rate by a rule written first, and H-21 asks before any run whether H-20's schedule then keeps its inhibitory sum at half the image's or more at every block's end in both arms while still learning every mapping with its couplings bounded, with its stopping rule; brief 054 runs it once, nothing of the engine changed |
| [ADR-0129](adr/0129-a-target-the-network-fires-at-measured.md) | A target the network fires at, measured: brief 054 — ADR-0128's rule, committed before it ran, read ADR-0077's settled image frozen under the reference drive at 18 449 spikes over $2^{20}$ ticks, 1.718 Hz a unit, and gave the inhibitory rule's target period as $\text{units} \cdot \text{ticks} / \text{spikes}$ rounded, 58 201 ticks, a depression of 23 per spike against the default's 67; the image each arm decodes H-20's with the period written and nothing else, by a masked check; the eleven whole-domain tests through the harness's run reproduced first; **H-21 is yes**: over H-20's schedule the inhibitory sum stayed at or above 0.848 of the settled image's at every block's end in both arms (H-20's fell to 0.072 and 0.078) and every mapping was learned, 113 to 124 of each mapping's last 128, no coupling past 1.30, the highest 1.190 and 1.193; the sum rose to 1.01, then fell, still falling at the end by about 0.15 per cent of the image's a block; the inhibition onto the stimulus units, at 4.5 Hz, rose to the rail, onto the inhibitory units and the readouts, at 2.2 and 1.75 Hz, fell to 0.65 and 0.82; F-59, the settled image's inhibition already below the rail, 45 of 6 528 synapses at it, 833 and 856 at the end; the reversal speeds H-20's within a block but for one; H-21's stopping rule at step 3, the next decision (the operating regime, another size, a critic of the engine's own) named and not taken |
| [ADR-0130](adr/0130-a-critic-of-the-engines-own.md) | A critic of the engine's own: after H-21, the learning line takes the last of H-20's open questions it named — the expected reward the dopamine signal is measured against, held today by the task and indexed by the stimulus the task says it presented, is held by the engine instead as a value weight on every unit in the record's last reserved bytes, the value read as the weights times each unit's spikes since the previous reward from the engine's own train, the error the reward less the value delivered to the modulator and each weight moved by the error times its unit's spikes, the delta rule of a linear actor–critic; its constants a parameter of the image, unset every run the run it was, format 19; H-22 written before any run: on H-21's configuration with the task's critic replaced by the engine's, every mapping learned, every coupling bounded and the engine's expectation at least half the reward on the correct trials, in both arms; brief 055 builds it and runs H-22 once |
| [ADR-0131](adr/0131-the-critic-built.md) | The critic built: ADR-0130's value weight as `value_weight`, an `i16` at the unit record's `[52..54)`, its last reserved bytes; the constants — a step's shift of at most 30 and a weight's scale of at most 14 — a parameter of the image at the modulator section's `[48..51)`, refused outside them and without a train; the value, the error and the step a pure rule in `cortex-neuromod`; the features counted as the coordinator merges each tick's spikes into the train; `Executor::reward` forming and delivering the error and moving every weight between ticks while the critic is set, and the reward itself while it is not; the discovery loop's own reward to the modulator itself; a unit with spikes pending kept from the sweep; a task with a critic of its own refused on an engine with one; format 19; unset, every run is the run it was, and the four whole-image pins re-pinned with the format under a masked check |
| [ADR-0132](adr/0132-a-critic-of-the-engines-own-measured.md) | A critic of the engine's own, measured: brief 055's protocol, committed before any rewarded run — the critic's step shift 9 and scale 2 placed by an arithmetic written first, the least sum of the two at which one stimulus volley moves the value of the same volley by no more than the task critic's thirty-second of the error, 51/2 048; H-21's image with the critic written and nothing else, by a masked check, and H-21's first block reproduced with the critic unset before it; H-21's two arms run once from it with the task's critic replaced by the engine's; H-22's clauses as integer rules and the readings beside them. **H-22 is no, on clause 3 alone**: every mapping learned in both arms, 89 to 125 of each mapping's last 128, no coupling past 1.30, the highest 1.186 and 1.185; the engine's value on each mapping's last correct trials at 0.495 to 0.935 of the reward, below the half in one mapping of eight; the value per stimulus about $2p - 1$ of the reward, a stimulus's expected reward, so that for such a critic the clause is an accuracy of three quarters (F-61); after the first and third flips the value fell to −0.92 to −1.03 and those reversals took 22 to 30 blocks against H-21's 14 to 23; step 5, and the next decision, an ADR on the critic's scale or window, named and not taken |

| [ADR-0134](adr/0134-the-critics-window-built.md) | The critic's window built: ADR-0133's window as `Config::critic_window_ticks`, a length in ticks at the modulator section's `[52..54)`, zero unset; a spike counted, where ADR-0131 counts it, only when its tick is fewer than the length after the tick the window opened at — the previous reward the critic took, or the engine's start, its build or its load; the length read from the image by `shortest_delay`, the least delay of any slot that holds a synapse, and the loader refusing a window that is not that length and a window without the critic; format 20; unset, every run is the run it was, and the five whole-image pins re-pinned with the format under a masked check |
| [ADR-0135](adr/0135-the-critics-window-measured.md) | The critic's window, measured: H-23 is yes — with the engine's critic counting only within 100 ticks after each reward, the shortest delay of any synapse the image carries, read by its rule and committed before any run with H-22's shift 9 and scale 2, H-22's schedule learned every mapping in both arms, 115 to 124 of each mapping's last 128, no coupling past 1.30, each reversal passed 40 of 64 in 14 to 20 blocks, within the task critic's 23, and each stimulus's mean value stood within 0.12 of the reward of 2p − 1; the prediction held; the window admitted the volley and about 1.6 background spikes a trial, the value rested on each stimulus's own units and came back after each flip as the task critic's did; the next decision, by H-23's stopping rule, among the operating regime, another size, the rule held by the network reopened and a critic carried by a population, not taken |

| [ADR-0143](adr/0143-the-gates-output-delivered.md) | The gate's output delivered: after H-26, the competition of the second step taken as the basal ganglia's output reaching the network — the selection made at the readout window's close, and the channel not selected inhibited from there to tick 2^12 of the trial, the span in which the pair rule still pairs the volley, by a schedule of negative basal messages derived from the membrane's rule and checked on a frozen run; the address's targets then released to every unit, its sources still drawn by the engine; a parameter of the task, unset every run the run it was; H-27 written before any run: on H-25's configuration with both, H-25's four clauses in both arms, no prediction for the verdict since the pair rule reads the target's last spike and the inhibition follows the response, and the release alone as the control, predicted no; brief 060 builds it and runs H-27 once |

| [ADR-0150](adr/0150-a-round-waits-for-what-it-checks.md) | A round waits for what it checks: a measuring round took seven hours and twenty minutes from its brief to its merge, seventy minutes of it the runs it was for; four waits that check nothing more are removed and no check is — the merge waits for the dispatch's whole-domain shards and not for its sweep, whose outcome the next decision's ADR records and whose survivors are findings; an ADR cites its round's commits as the pull request holds them and is set accepted by the commit that asks for the merge, so that no second pull request follows; the arms run once on the developer machine and the dispatch is their reproduction; the whole-domain tests are dealt to twelve shards; the calibration before any rewarded run weighed and kept; `CLAUDE.md`'s account of the tree returned to a switchboard's length; no rule of the engine, no test and no pinned number changed |

## 10. Quality requirements

### 10.1 Quality tree

```text
Quality
├── Determinism ── T-1 cross-platform bit-exactness
├── Memory density ── T-2 reference footprint (Appendix A)
├── Latency ── T-3 per-event dispatch cost
├── Real-time embodiment ── T-4 period · T-5 jitter
├── Availability of ingress ── T-6 hot-plug pause
├── Start-up ── T-7 cold boot
└── Verifiability ── V-1 layout assertions · V-2 executable docs · V-3 decision graph · V-4 briefs · V-5 manifests · V-6 mutation · V-7 determinism on two architectures (Appendix B)
```

### 10.2 Scenarios and targets

Every row is a **Target** unless its "Measured" column has a value. A measurement is admissible only when it is produced by a benchmark in this repository on the reference platform of §7.1 and is reproducible from a committed command ([ADR-0010](adr/0010-measured-or-target.md)).

| ID | Stimulus | Response measure | Target | Measured | Protocol |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| T-1 | Same image, seed and input trace on x86-64-v4 and ARMv9-A | SHA-256 of all arenas after $10^6$ ticks | identical | — | Differential test in CI on two runners. The same-platform form, one worker against four over a 128-unit network with STDP, exists (`runtime/cortex-runtime/tests/differential.rs`, [ADR-0023](adr/0023-executor.md)); the two-architecture form at 20 000 ticks with a pinned CRC-64 runs in CI on `ubuntu-24.04` and `ubuntu-24.04-arm` ([ADR-0030](adr/0030-verification-governance.md), Implemented). |
| T-2 | Reference configuration of Appendix A loaded | Resident set size | ≤ 20 GB local DRAM | — | `/proc/self/status` `VmRSS` after load; huge pages enabled. |
| T-3 | One spike enqueued to a hot unit on an isolated core | Enqueue latency (R-1 steps 1–3) | median < 20 ns, p99.99 < 50 ns | — | `criterion` micro-benchmark plus `perf stat`; 10⁸ samples; isolated core, fixed frequency. |
| T-4 | Embodiment loop against a physics stub | Period | 1.000 ms | — | Timestamps in the shared ring over 10⁶ periods. |
| T-5 | Same | Jitter | p99.9 ≤ 20 µs; p100 ≤ 100 µs | — | Same; requires `isolcpus` and `nohz_full`. |
| T-6 | Driver swapped while ingesting at full rate | Simulation-loop stall | 0 ticks lost | — | Tick counter continuity across 10³ swaps. |
| T-7 | Reference image opened from NVMe | Time to first tick | warm page cache: bounded by the copy and the bitwise checksum until `mmap` (§6.7; no figure is claimed); cold: bounded by device bandwidth (≈ 1 s at 12 GB/s for an 11 GB image) | — | Wall clock around R-7 with `MAP_POPULATE`. |
| T-8 | Sustained random spiking at 2 % activity | Delivered spikes per second per node | > 10⁸ | — | Throughput benchmark; report with T-3 conditions. |

Earlier revisions of this document stated stronger figures (for example "P99.99 < 35 ns" and "< 100 ms cold boot of 86 billion nodes") as achievements. They were never measured and are withdrawn; the targets above are the ones the design is expected to reach, with their preconditions stated.

**Benchmarks.** `benches/cortex-bench` ([ADR-0014](adr/0014-benchmark-harness.md)) measures the parts of T-3 that exist (the wheel, the mailbox and gate, integration, plasticity, fan-out and STDP, the voice, one message through the executor's three phases, and the tick that does nothing on one, two and four workers, [ADR-0035](adr/0035-cadence-and-the-population-tally.md); the list is the benchmarks README's), each from a deterministic input sequence. The protocol for an admissible run and the results convention are in [`docs/benchmarks/README.md`](benchmarks/README.md); every results file begins with an `admissible:` line, and only an admissible file may be cited in the Measured column. No admissible run exists yet, so the column stays empty; the two recorded runs are developer-machine figures marked not admissible.

<!-- @assert-present file="benches/cortex-bench/Cargo.toml,benches/cortex-bench/benches/hot_path.rs,docs/benchmarks/README.md" -->
